# Kain Stdlib Map

Generated by `kain stdlib-map`. Do not edit by hand.

## Import Rules

- Prefer root-domain imports such as `use std::fs`, `use std::hash`, `use std::net`, and `use std::ui`.
- `@extern` symbols are ABI-facing declarations; prefer public wrappers unless you are changing runtime substrate.
- Visibility is recorded. Private helpers are listed so agents can understand implementation shape, but public symbols are the authoring surface.

## Cook By Need

### Read/write files and paths

- import: `std::fs`
- symbols: `fs_append_text`, `fs_atomic_write_text`, `fs_read_text`, `fs_read_text_range`, `fs_write_text`, `native_fs_last_error_kind`, `native_fs_last_error_message`, `native_fs_last_status`

### Spawn processes and capture output

- import: `std::process`
- symbols: `process_platform_available`, `process_reset`, `process_spec_count`, `process_spec_create`, `process_spec_destroy`, `process_stdio_inherit`, `process_stdio_null`, `process_stdio_pipe`

### Open network/http/tls lanes

- import: `std::net`
- symbols: `net_capability_available`, `net_capability_state`, `net_capability_supported`, `net_platform_available`, `net_platform_name`, `net_reset`, `tcp_connect`, `tcp_listen`

### Build deterministic hashes and fingerprints

- import: `std::hash`
- symbols: `HASH_BYTE_MASK`, `HASH_CRC32_POLY`, `HASH_EMPTY32`, `HASH_FNV1A32_OFFSET`, `HASH_FNV1A32_PRIME`, `HASH_GOLDEN32`, `HASH_U32_MASK`, `Hash32`

### Use actors and mailbox helpers

- import: `std::actor`
- symbols: `native_actor_abi_version`, `native_actor_default_ask_timeout_ms`, `native_actor_default_mailbox_capacity`, `native_actor_default_shutdown_grace_ms`, `native_actor_invalid_id`, `native_actor_supervision_max_restarts`, `native_actor_supervision_restart_window_millis`, `native_actor_unbounded_mailbox_capacity`

### Create native graphics sessions

- import: `std::graphics`
- symbols: `native_graphics_backend_available`, `native_graphics_backend_select`, `native_graphics_backend_status`, `native_graphics_backend_supported`, `native_graphics_reset`, `native_graphics_session_count`, `native_graphics_session_create`, `native_graphics_session_destroy`

### Author UI handles and component helpers

- import: `std::ui`
- symbols: `native_ui_begin_frame`, `native_ui_end_frame`, `native_ui_reset`, `native_ui_session_count`, `native_ui_session_create`, `native_ui_session_destroy`, `native_ui_window_close`, `native_ui_window_open`

### Use math vectors, matrices, quaternions, and colors

- import: `std::math`
- symbols: `DEG_TO_RAD`, `EPSILON`, `HALF_PI`, `HUGE_EPSILON`, `PI`, `RAD_TO_DEG`, `TAU`, `pi`

### `std::hash`

- source: `stdlib/hash.kn`
- parse: `parsed`
- module docs: Canonical root hashing stdlib for Kain.  This module is intentionally target-neutral Kain source: deterministic 32-bit word mixing, byte-fed FNV-1a, CRC32 byte folding, and fingerprint combinators that are useful to capsules, caches, wire formats, benchmarks, and compiler/runtime proof blades without depending on host string layout.

### `std::math`

- source: `stdlib/math.kn`
- parse: `parsed`
- module docs: Canonical root math stdlib for Kain.  This is the engine-facing math surface for native LLVM, shaders, UI, graphics, simulation, procedural generation, and GPU layout authoring. It favors explicit data layouts and free functions so the surface stays stable across Kain's current compiler/runtime lanes.

## Rust Builtins

- `fn abs(x: Int) -> Int` - Absolute value
- `fn ask(actor: ActorRef, message: String, request: Any) -> Any` - Send a message to an actor and wait for the first reply
- `fn ask_timeout(actor: ActorRef, message: String, request: Any, timeout_ms: Int) -> Any` - Send a message to an actor and wait for the first reply with a custom timeout
- `fn assert(condition: Bool, message: String) -> Unit` - Assert condition
- `fn byte_at(s: String, index: Int) -> Int` - Read a raw byte value at a string index, or -1 when out of range
- `fn ceil(x: Float) -> Int` - Ceiling
- `fn char_at(s: String, index: Int) -> String` - Read a single character at an index as a string
- `fn chr(codepoint: Int) -> String` - Create a single-character string from a Unicode scalar value
- `fn clamp(x: Int, lo: Int, hi: Int) -> Int` - Clamp between bounds
- `fn command_run(program: String, args: Array<String>, workdir: String) -> CommandRunResult` - Run a subprocess, capture stdout/stderr, and return the exit status
- `fn contains(s: String, sub: String) -> Bool` - Check contains
- `fn copy_file(src: String, dest: String) -> Unit` - Copy one file to another path
- `fn cos(x: Float) -> Float` - Cosine
- `fn create_dir_all(path: String) -> Unit` - Create a directory and any missing parents
- `fn cross(a: Vec3, b: Vec3) -> Vec3` - Cross product
- `fn cwd() -> String` - Return the current working directory
- `fn dbg(value: Any) -> Any` - Debug print and return
- `fn distance(a: Vec3, b: Vec3) -> Float` - Distance between points
- `fn dot(a: Vec3, b: Vec3) -> Float` - Dot product
- `fn ends_with(s: String, suffix: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a string ends with a suffix
- `fn env(name: String) -> String` - Read an environment variable, returning an empty string if missing
- `fn file_exists(path: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a path exists
- `fn filter(array: Array, fn: Function) -> Array` - Filter array
- `fn find_substring_from(s: String, needle: String, start: Int) -> Int` - Find the first substring occurrence at or after a byte offset
- `fn floor(x: Float) -> Int` - Floor
- `fn fs_append_bytes(path: String, bytes: Array<Int>) -> Unit` - Append raw bytes to a file
- `fn fs_append_text(path: String, content: String) -> Unit` - Append UTF-8 text to a file
- `fn fs_atomic_write_bytes(path: String, bytes: Array<Int>) -> Unit` - Write raw bytes through an atomic sibling temp path
- `fn fs_atomic_write_text(path: String, content: String) -> Unit` - Write UTF-8 text through an atomic sibling temp path
- `fn fs_capability_describe() -> String` - Describe current filesystem sandbox mounts and grants
- `fn fs_capability_grant(capability: String) -> Unit` - Mutate the current filesystem capability set
- `fn fs_capability_has(capability: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a filesystem capability is granted
- `fn fs_capability_revoke(capability: String) -> Unit` - Mutate the current filesystem capability set
- `fn fs_copy_file(src: String, dest: String) -> Int` - Copy one file
- `fn fs_copy_file_streaming(src: String, dest: String, chunk_size: Int) -> Int` - Copy a file through a bounded streaming buffer
- `fn fs_copy_path(src: String, dest: String) -> Unit` - Copy a file or directory tree
- `fn fs_create_dir(path: String) -> Unit` - Create one directory
- `fn fs_create_dir_all(path: String) -> Unit` - Create a directory and any missing parents
- `fn fs_exists(path: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a path exists
- `fn fs_glob(path: String) -> Array<String>` - Expand a glob pattern into sorted paths
- `fn fs_hash_file(path: String) -> String` - Compute a SHA-256 hash for a file
- `fn fs_is_dir(path: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a path is a directory
- `fn fs_is_file(path: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a path is a file
- `fn fs_is_symlink(path: String) -> Bool` - Check whether a path is a symlink
- `fn fs_metadata(path: String) -> FsMetadata` - Read typed metadata for a path
- `fn fs_metadata_text(path: String) -> String` - Read native metadata as stable key-value text
- `fn fs_mount(key: String, root: String, mode: String) -> Unit` - Mount a host path under an fs:// virtual root
- `fn fs_move_path(src: String, dest: String) -> Unit` - Move or rename a path
- `fn fs_path_absolute(path: String) -> String` - Return an absolute normalized path
- `fn fs_path_canonicalize(path: String) -> String` - Return the filesystem canonical path
- `fn fs_path_extension(path: String) -> String` - Return the path extension or empty string
- `fn fs_path_file_name(path: String) -> String` - Return the final path component or empty string
- `fn fs_path_join(src: String, dest: String) -> String` - Join two path segments
- `fn fs_path_normalize(path: String) -> String` - Normalize a path lexically
- `fn fs_path_parent(path: String) -> String` - Return the parent directory path or empty string
- `fn fs_path_stem(path: String) -> String` - Return the path stem or empty string
- `fn fs_read_byte_range_hex(path: String, offset: Int, length: Int) -> String` - Read a file byte range as lowercase hex
- `fn fs_read_bytes(path: String) -> Array<Int>` - Read raw bytes from a file
- `fn fs_read_bytes_hex(path: String) -> String` - Read raw file bytes as a lowercase hex string
- `fn fs_read_bytes_range(path: String, offset: Int, length: Int) -> Array<Int>` - Read a byte range from a file
- `fn fs_read_dir(path: String) -> Array<FsDirEntry>` - Read direct directory entries as FsDirEntry values
- `fn fs_read_dir_paths(path: String) -> Array<String>` - Read direct directory entries as sorted paths
- `fn fs_read_dir_paths_text(path: String) -> String` - Read direct directory paths as newline-delimited text
- `fn fs_read_text(path: String) -> String` - Read UTF-8 text from a file
- `fn fs_read_text_range(path: String, offset: Int, length: Int) -> String` - Read a UTF-8 byte range from a file
- `fn fs_remove_dir(path: String) -> Unit` - Remove an empty directory
- `fn fs_remove_dir_all(path: String) -> Unit` - Remove a directory tree
- `fn fs_remove_file(path: String) -> Unit` - Remove a file
- `fn fs_remove_path(path: String) -> Unit` - Remove a file or directory tree if it exists
- `fn fs_resolve(path: String) -> String` - Resolve a virtual or host path through the filesystem sandbox
- `fn fs_sandbox_allow_host_paths(allow: Bool) -> Unit` - Allow or deny direct host paths outside virtual roots
- `fn fs_stream_chunks(path: String, chunk_size: Int) -> Array<FsChunk>` - Read a file as deterministic byte chunks
- `fn fs_symlink_metadata(path: String) -> FsMetadata` - Read symlink-aware metadata for a path
- `fn fs_temp_dir(path: String) -> String` - Create a temp directory and return its path
- `fn fs_temp_file(path: String) -> String` - Create a temp file and return its path
- `fn fs_try_append_bytes(path: String, bytes: Array<Int>) -> Any` - Append raw bytes as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_append_text(path: String, content: String) -> Any` - Append UTF-8 text as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_atomic_write_text(path: String, content: String) -> Any` - Atomic text write as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_copy_file(src: String, dest: String) -> Any` - Copy one file as Result<Int, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_copy_path(src: String, dest: String) -> Any` - Copy a path as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_create_dir(path: String) -> Any` - Create one directory as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_create_dir_all(path: String) -> Any` - Create missing parent directories as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_glob(path: String) -> Any` - Expand a glob pattern as Result<Array<String>, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_hash_file(path: String) -> Any` - Compute a SHA-256 hash as Result<String, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_metadata(path: String) -> Any` - Read typed metadata as Result<FsMetadata, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_move_path(src: String, dest: String) -> Any` - Move or rename a path as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_read_bytes(path: String) -> Any` - Read raw bytes from a file as Result<Array<Int>, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_read_dir(path: String) -> Any` - Read direct directory entries as Result<Array<FsDirEntry>, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_read_dir_paths(path: String) -> Any` - Read direct directory paths as Result<Array<String>, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_read_text(path: String) -> Any` - Read UTF-8 text from a file as Result<String, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_remove_dir(path: String) -> Any` - Remove an empty directory as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_remove_dir_all(path: String) -> Any` - Remove a directory tree as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_remove_file(path: String) -> Any` - Remove a file as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_remove_path(path: String) -> Any` - Remove a path as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_symlink_metadata(path: String) -> Any` - Read symlink-aware metadata as Result<FsMetadata, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_walk(path: String) -> Any` - Recursively walk a directory as Result<Array<FsDirEntry>, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_write_bytes(path: String, bytes: Array<Int>) -> Any` - Write raw bytes as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_try_write_text(path: String, content: String) -> Any` - Write UTF-8 text as Result<Unit, FsError>
- `fn fs_tx_append_text(transaction_id: Int, path: String, content: String) -> Unit` - Queue a transactional text operation
- `fn fs_tx_begin() -> Int` - Begin a filesystem transaction
- `fn fs_tx_commit(transaction_id: Int) -> Array<FsJournalEntry>` - Finish a filesystem transaction and return its journal
- `fn fs_tx_copy_path(transaction_id: Int, src: String, dest: String) -> Unit` - Queue a transactional two-path operation
- `fn fs_tx_move_path(transaction_id: Int, src: String, dest: String) -> Unit` - Queue a transactional two-path operation
- `fn fs_tx_remove_path(transaction_id: Int, path: String) -> Unit` - Queue a transactional path removal
- `fn fs_tx_rollback(transaction_id: Int) -> Array<FsJournalEntry>` - Finish a filesystem transaction and return its journal
- `fn fs_tx_write_text(transaction_id: Int, path: String, content: String) -> Unit` - Queue a transactional text operation
- `fn fs_unmount(key: String) -> Bool` - Unmount an fs:// virtual root

- `fn input_action_down(session_id: Int, action: String) -> Int` - Return 1 when an action is held after this frame

- `fn input_action_released(session_id: Int, action: String) -> Int` - Return 1 when an action was released this frame

- `fn input_bind_action(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, event_kind: String, code: String, action: String) -> Int` - Bind a source event to a semantic action
- `fn input_bind_axis(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, event_kind: String, code: String, axis: String, scale: Float) -> Int` - Bind a source event to a semantic axis
- `fn input_push_agent_intent(session_id: Int, source_id: String, action: String, command_text: String, confidence: Float) -> Int` - Push a first-class agent intent as a momentary action
- `fn input_push_event(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, source_id: String, event_kind: String, code: String, value: Float, text: String, confidence: Float) -> Int` - Push a typed input event into a session

- `fn json_get_bool(object: Any, key: String) -> Bool` - Read a required boolean field from a JSON object
- `fn json_get_float(object: Any, key: String) -> Float` - Read a required floating-point field from a JSON object
- `fn json_get_int(object: Any, key: String) -> Int` - Read a required integer field from a JSON object
- `fn json_get_string(object: Any, key: String) -> String` - Read a required string field from a JSON object

- `fn json_object_set(object: Any, key: String, value: Any) -> Unit` - Set a field on a mutable JSON object
- `fn json_parse(text: String) -> Any` - Parse JSON text
- `fn json_string(value: Any) -> String` - Serialize a KAIN value into JSON text

- `fn kain_input_bind_action(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, event_kind: String, code: String, action: String) -> Int` - Bridge: bind input action
- `fn kain_input_bind_axis(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, event_kind: String, code: String, axis: String, scale: Float) -> Int` - Bridge: bind input axis
- `fn kain_input_event_action(session_id: Int, index: Int) -> String` - Bridge: inspect current input event action
- `fn kain_input_event_code(session_id: Int, index: Int) -> String` - Bridge: inspect current input event code
- `fn kain_input_event_count(session_id: Int) -> Int` - Bridge: query current input event count
- `fn kain_input_event_kind(session_id: Int, index: Int) -> String` - Bridge: inspect current input event kind
- `fn kain_input_event_source_kind(session_id: Int, index: Int) -> String` - Bridge: inspect current input event source
- `fn kain_input_event_text(session_id: Int, index: Int) -> String` - Bridge: inspect current input event text
- `fn kain_input_frame_index(session_id: Int) -> Int` - Bridge: query current input frame index
- `fn kain_input_push_agent_intent(session_id: Int, source_id: String, action: String, command_text: String, confidence: Float) -> Int` - Bridge: push agent intent
- `fn kain_input_push_event(session_id: Int, source_kind: String, source_id: String, event_kind: String, code: String, value: Float, text: String, confidence: Float) -> Int` - Bridge: push input event

## Native Runtime Services

- `actor.registry`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Named actor registration and lookup
- `actor.runtime`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Actor spawn, mailbox, lifecycle, and scheduling
- `asset.gltf`: `degraded` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - The legacy runtime-owned glTF loader was removed from the active runtime; authored packages or blade-owned loaders must satisfy this seam
- `asset.ingestion`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Canonical descriptor-driven entry path for assets and emitted bundles
- `asset.realtime`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Realtime bundle loading and scene management
- `async.runtime`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Task/future execution, wake/poll, and cancellation
- `async.timers`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Timer registration and wake delivery
- `base.diagnostics`: `available` `required` on `all declared platforms` - Structured diagnostics and error reporting
- `base.memory`: `available` `required` on `all declared platforms` - Core allocation, retain/release, and memory management
- `compatibility`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Version validation, migration, hot reload, and snapshot flow
- `contract`: `available` `required` on `all declared platforms` - Runtime contract bundle loading and validation
- `control.converge.autotune`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Proof-bounded native converge lane selection, warm cache state, and fixed-ring telemetry for future silicon autotuning
- `cpu.capabilities`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Runtime-published CPU feature bits for compiled converge selectors, including x86 AVX/AVX2/AVX-512 gates
- `data.json`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Native JSON parse/render/object/array helpers for LLVM builtin calls over string, integer, boolean, null, object, and array values
- `device.reflection`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Backend, GPU, display, and hotplug capability descriptors
- `gfx.backend.d3d12`: `degraded` `optional` on `windows` - Runtime-owned DirectX 12 backend identity and capability target; concrete presenters live outside the C runtime
- `gfx.backend.vulkan`: `degraded` `optional` on `windows, linux` - Runtime-owned Vulkan backend identity and capability target; concrete presenters live outside the C runtime
- `gfx.compute`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Compute bundle validation, dispatch planning, and native runtime handoff
- `gfx.raw-native`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Catalog-free graphics kernel for Kain-authored engines, buffers, SPIR-V modules, pipelines, and draw commands
- `gfx.shader.spirv`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Canonical native shader payload registration for Kain-authored graphics and compute pipelines
- `gfx.viewport`: `degraded` `optional` on `windows` - Platform window handles and presenter attachment contract; concrete presenters now live in blades or packages
- `host.bridge`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Plugin and foreign service integration
- `io.net`: `available` `optional` on `windows, linux` - Native TCP, protocol-aware HTTP client/server, capability-query, and Windows-first HTTPS/HTTP2 client primitives
- `io.process`: `available` `optional` on `windows, linux` - Native child-process, pipe, and PTY session management
- `machine.stones`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Native axiom predicates, pulse scheduler/snapshots, shatter SoA lane buffers, and teleport zero-copy handoff telemetry
- `memory.ownership`: `available` `required` on `all declared platforms` - Native collapse/observe/decay guards for helper-owned heap regions and imported pointer lifetimes
- `platform.app-host`: `available` `required` on `windows` - Raw Win32 app/window host substrate without baked presenters or app policy
- `platform.input`: `available` `required` on `windows` - Canonical Kain input sessions, semantic actions, replay traces, and native platform event handling
- `reflection`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Reflection payload loading and runtime type lookup
- `runtime.inspection`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Runtime-owned scene, resource, and binding inspection queries
- `scene.mutation`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Transactional scene mutation requests and receipts
- `scene.query`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Picking, raycast, bounds, visibility, and selection query contracts
- `scene.runtime`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Stable native scene handles and runtime-owned scene state access
- `ui.bundle`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Compiled UI bundle loading and overlay rendering
- `ui.component`: `available` `optional` on `all declared platforms` - Component state, invalidation, focus, and event routing
